# 现行技能明确引用的原件复核

引用位置来自 2c165f1 的冻结技能。每条记录把使用方式与本机原件核对。原件身份、引用假设及实际证明依赖分别记载。文献出现的页码不等于全文验证。

本版登记 371 份原件。记录状态按所查范围确定。

## e8f8d143f231 · On the Bloch and Q_p--Carleson measure problems

- 原件。2026 preprint PDF, 30 pp.。PDF 物理页 1, 9, 11, 25, 29。SHA-256 e8f8d143f231a4b3092291a852cd7d8629a61bbf171d73f07eb45322e12e7e0f。
- 关联入口。math-g01-carleson-measure-box-and-tent-test, math-g02-outer-lp-size-and-tree-stopping, math-g03-directional-capture-tree-quadratic-audit, math-g05-tree-carleson-square-function-tc-a4-audit。
- 原文位置。p.9 Lemma 2.2（˚B={f∈B:f(0)=0} 的表示）；pp.11--12 Theorem 3.1；pp.25--26 Theorem 6.6；p.29 Theorem 6.9。
- 当前用法。G01/G02/G03/G05 仅把它作为受合同约束的 Bloch/Q_p 嵌入来源，并明确禁止把 ˚B 误称为 little Bloch。
- 限域采用。匹配：原文的 ˚B 是零点归一化 Bloch 子空间，且相应定理确为特定 dyadic/p-admissible resolution 下的嵌入刻画；当前文字没有把其推广为任意树的 Carleson 结论。依据。Lemma 2.2 与 Theorems 3.1、6.6、6.9。

Theorem 4.2 is exactly for a random labeling of G_{n,n} conditioned on one peak; it bounds the event that at least f(n)n² prefixes have exterior boundary at most n^(2−f(n)) by exp(−½n²(log log n)²). It neither treats two peaks on that square nor yields an arbitrary graph result.。依据。Theorem 4.2's denominator/numerator counting argument; free-boundary square and single-peak conditioning are essential.。

## 787a427d3f58 · On the Fourth Moment Condition for Rademacher Chaos

- 原件。arXiv:1706.00751v3 (2017-10-09)。PDF 物理页 4, 7。SHA-256 787a427d3f582b5c4fea2cf12942b2990d233aa9642ba85c35887c5f6495f1bd。
